Algorithm per restart:
1. p initial.
2. Compute score.
3. For iteration until time:
   a. Compute c, active weights.
   b. Compute g centered scaled.
   c. Try a set of step sizes η (e.g., 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0) maybe adapt to norm(g). For each, p_cand = softmax(log p - η g) (or p*exp), normalize, floor.
   d. Compute score for each (can reuse c? c changes, need FFT). If any improves, pick best improving (or best surrogate) and apply; continue. If none, reduce step or random leap.
Accepting only improvements makes it a local search; with deterministic gradient, may stall. Need periodic random perturbations: add Gaussian to y with scale maybe 0.5, or block multiply exp(N(0,σ)). If no improvement for a while, do larger leap.

Let's consider the "state of art" LP approach: Given f, solve LP to maximize sum g subject to f*g ≤ M. If f normalized sum sqrt(2n), M=||f*f||∞. Let's understand the LP iteration. Normalize f: sum f = sqrt(2n). M = max(f*f). Solve:
max 1^T g
s.t. A g ≤ M 1, g≥0,
where A_{k,j} = sum_i f_i f? Wait constraint (f*g)_k ≤ M. Convolution (f*g)_k = ∑_i f_i g_{k-i}. This is linear in g with coefficients f_i. Matrix A is a banded Toeplitz? For each k, coefficients are f over valid indices. It has at most n nonzeros per row but overall O(n^2). A is not symmetric? It is Toeplitz with f. The LP maximizes sum g. The optimal g has support where constraints tight. Then rescale g to sum sqrt(2n), update f = (1-t)f + t g. This may reduce max.

Let's derive an analytic construction using **alternating signs in convolution of signed sequence**. If we take a signed sequence s with small square coefficients (e.g., Barker/Rudin-Shapiro) and then convert to nonnegative by p_i = (A + s_i)/B? The square coefficients include s*s which can be negative in correction, allowing cancellation of the uniform part! This is key. Even though p_i nonnegative, p_i p_j = (A+s_i)(A+s_j)/B^2 = (A^2 + A s_i + A s_j + s_i s_j). The s_i s_j coefficients can be negative, canceling A^2 uniform contributions. For nonnegative p, p_i p_j ≥0, so total coefficients nonnegative, but the signed part can reduce from uniform. To get constant reduction, s_i s_j coefficients must be O(n) negative. This requires s_i entries large O(√n) or pattern with non-zero mean? Let's set p_i = (1 + r_i)/n with r_i ≥ -1. Uniform base 1/n. Coeff =1/n [1 + ε terms + (r_i r_j)/n?]. If r_i can be negative down to -1 and positive up to? To get O(n) product sum for a lag, r_i r_{k-i} sum must be O(n) with coefficient 1/n^2 times n? p square coefficient = (1/n^2)∑ (1+r_i)(1+r_j). Uniform part L_k/n^2 ≈1/n. The signed sum ∑ r_i r_j can be O(n) if r_i have nonzero correlation. Since r_i bounded? Lower -1, upper can be large but if upper large sparse, product sums perhaps O(√n). To get O(n), r_i must have low-frequency structure with nonzero mean over sliding windows; but sum r=0. It can have blocky structure. However blocky increases some lags positive; we can use signed sequence r with **negative autocorrelation for all lags** of magnitude O(n). Does such bounded nonnegative-lower sequence exist? This is a "low autocorrelation sequence" with negative average? The full sum over lags of ∑ r_i r_{k-i} = (∑r)^2=0, so not all lags negative; but can be negative for the lags that are maximum of uniform? Mirror optimizer function:
```
def optimize_mirror(n, init, max_time, seed, use_pal=False):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=normalize(init)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score(p)
    lr=0.8
    lr_power=?
    stall=0
    last_improve=0
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > max_time: break
       c=conv(p,p)
       M=c.max()
       # weights topK
       temp = max(M*0.15, 1e-12)
       # maybe choose K = max(10, min(2*n, max(50, int(n*0.2))))
       # use softmax over values above M - 5*temp? 
       weights = np.exp((c-M)/temp)
       weights[weights<1e-6]=0
       if sum weights <1e-12: weights = top K uniform
       idx = np.argsort(-c)[:K]  # ensure top included even if underflow
       # blend weights: softmax over top K indices
       w_val = np.exp((c[idx]-M)/temp); normalize
       idx_top = idx
       # g
       g=zeros(n)
       p_rev=p[::-1]; n1=n-1
       for k,w in zip(idx_top,w_val):
          lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n,k+1); L=hi-lo
          start=(n1-k)+lo
          g[lo:hi] += 2*w*p_rev[start:start+L]
       g -= g.mean()
       # scale g by maybe n? We included p; c ~1/n, g ~1/n. Need multiply by maybe target_score? Let's scale by best_sc? Use g *= best_sc / (np.nanstd(g)+1e-12) * 0.5? To make std ~lr.
       g_std = g.std()
       if g_std>0: g = g / g_std * 0.8  # step relative
       # try step in [-?]. Mirror update p = softmax(log p - eta g) with eta maybe 0.1,0.3,1,3
       for eta in [0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]:
          y=np.log(p+1e-300) - eta*g
          y=np.clip(y, -30,30)
          cand=softmax(y)
          sc=score(cand)
          if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-9):
              p=cand; best_p=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc; stall=0; break
       else:
          # accept best surrogate? or random perturbation
          # Apply a small move with eta=0.3 regardless? Maybe not.
          stall +=1
          if stall > 20:
              # random leap
              y=np.log(p+1e-300)+rng.normal(0, 0.5)
              y=np.clip(y,-20,20)
              p=softmax(y); stall=0
              # keep best separate, continue from p (may be worse)
    return best_p, best_sc
```
But if we always continue from p that may be worse after leap, maybe okay. However if no improving move, p unchanged except leap. This is local search with random restarts. Should find good if landscape okay.

For n even/odd, free indices 0..m-1 (m=(n+1)//2). p_full built from free: p_full[:m]=free; p_full[n-1:-m-1:-1]=free if even? For n even: first half length n/2, second mirror reversed first half (not including duplicate center?) Palindromic p length n: p[i]=p[n-1-i]. Independent m=ceil(n/2) if odd else n/2? For even, p[0:n/2] mirror fully: p[n/2:] = reverse(p[:n/2]). For odd, center independent. We can simply optimize full with constraint p = (p+rev)/2 every step; easier. Compute g, symmetrize g = (g + g[::-1])/2, update full, then symmetrize p=(p+p[::-1])/2 normalize. This may reduce but okay.

LP optimizer:
```
def optimize_lp(n, init, max_time, seed):
    rng=...
    f=normalize(init)*sqrt(2n)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_sc=score(p)
    t=0.1
    stall=0
    while time<max_time:
       M=score(p)  # max f*f
       # maybe if M < target*1.01 break
       A build
       res=linprog(-ones, A, M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
       if not success: break
       g=res.x
       s=g.sum()
       if s <= f.sum(): # no direction
           # random perturb or break
       g_rescaled=g*(f.sum()/s)
       for alpha in [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0]:
          fnew=(1-alpha)f+alpha*g_rescaled
          sc=score(fnew/sum)
          if sc<best: update; f=fnew; break
       else:
          # maybe reduce alpha, random
    return best
```

FFT score helper:
```
def convolution_lin(a, b=None):
   if b None: b=a; n=len(a)
   if n < 128: return np.convolve(a,b)
   L=1
   need=2*n-1
   while L<need: L*=2
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, L)[:need]
   return c
```
For square, one rfft.

def score_p(p):
   p=np.asarray(P, float); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
   p=p/s
   c=conv_square(p)
   return 2*p.size*max(c)
```

_DE_N=1; _DE_USE_SURROGATE=True; _DE_BETA=20
def de_objective(x):
   n=_DE_N
   y=np.clip(x, -20,20)
   p=np.exp(y-np.max(y)); p/=p.sum()
   c=conv_square(p)
   M=np.max(c)
   # use surrogate scaled to [0, maybe]
   return 2*n* (np.log(np.sum(np.exp(_DE_BETA*(c - M))))/ _DE_BETA)  # shifted by M to avoid overflow; actual objective surrogate
```

Implement `gradient_from_weights(p, q, use_fft=True)`:
```
n=len(p); Lq=2*n-1
q_rev = q[::-1]
if n < 256:
    conv = np.convolve(p, q_rev)  # length 3n-2
else:
    L=1; need=3*n-2; while L<need: L*=2
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); Q=np.fft.rfft(q_rev,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Q,L)[:3*n-2]
t0=2*n-2
# g_i=2*conv[t0-i] for i=0..n-1, i.e. conv[t0 : t0-n : -1]
h = conv[t0-n+1 : t0+1][::-1] # t0-n+1 = n-1; to t0
g=2*h
return g
```

def lb_value(x):

   # logsum stable

```
class LBContext:
    def __init__(self,n,beta,scale): ...
    def f_g(self,x): compute val,grad; store
    def value(self,x):
        if np.array_equal(x,self.x): return self.val
        self.f_g(x); return self.val
    def gradient(self,x):
        if np.array_equal(x,self.x): return self.grad
        self.f_g(x); return self.grad
```

Implement `conv_square`:
```
def conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    if n < 256:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       return c
    need=2*n-1
    L=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.zeros(L); fa[:n]=a
    F=np.fft.rfft(fa)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:need]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0.0
    return c
```
For n=1024, need=2047, L=2048; good.

`gradient_weights_fft(p,q)`:
q length 2n-1. For n<256, use np.convolve direct; else FFT size 3n-2.
```
def gradient_weights(p,q):
   n=p.size
   q_rev=q[::-1]
   need=3*n-2
   if n<256:
      conv=np.convolve(p,q_rev)
   else:
      L=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
      P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); Q=np.fft.rfft(q_rev,L)
      conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Q,L)[:need]
   # h_i = conv[2n-2-i]
   start = 2*n-2 - (n-1)  # n-1
   h = conv[start : 2*n-1][::-1]  # length n
   return 2.0*h
```
Check slice: start=n-1, stop exclusive 2n-1? Need indices n-1 through 2n-2 inclusive length n. `conv[start:2*n-1]` gives n-1..2n-2. Reverse. Good.

For n small direct. q may have length need; conv length 3n-2, indices up to 2n-2 valid. Good.

L-BFGS objective for n small uses `conv_square` exact. For large, FFT. Need ensure p no NaN.

Random initial y functions top-level:
```
def palindromize_y(y_full): return 0.5*(y_full+y_full[::-1])
def make_y_random(n, rng, mode, scale=1.0):
   y=np.zeros(n)
   if mode==0: y += rng.normal(0,scale,n); y = palindromize_y(y)
   elif mode==1:
      m=(n+1)//2
      # create smooth random spectral in half
      y_half = rng.normal(0, scale, m)
      y_half = palindromize? For y_full symmetric, just y_full = palindromize(random)
   elif mode==2: # Fourier cosine symmetric
      m=n
      t=np.arange(n)
      y = rng.normal(0, scale, n)
      # low pass: keep first few Fourier modes? Since symmetric, use cosine only.
      if n>1:
         for k in range(1, min(5,n)):
            amp=scale/max(1,k*0.5)
            phase=rng.uniform(0,np.pi)
            y += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*t/(n-1)+phase)
      y=palindromize_y(y)
   else: # Dirichlet-ish via gammas
      alpha = 0.3 + rng.uniform(0,2)
      g = rng.gamma(alpha, size=n)
      y = np.log(g)
      y = palindromize_y(y)
   # avoid huge: center and clip
   y -= y.mean()
   y = np.clip(y, -6, 6)
   return y
```
But for symmetric random, `palindromize_y` of n odd works: y_full and reverse equal? For any array, 0.5(y+y[::-1]) is palindromic. Good.

Mirror polish function:
```
def polish_mirror(p, time_limit, rng):
    n=p.size; p=p.copy(); best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    lr_scale=1.0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
       c=conv_square(p); M=c.max()
       temp=max(M*0.1,1e-12)
       # weights softmax top all? Use full softmax and FFT gradient or topK loops.
       q=np.exp((c-M)/temp); qsum=q.sum(); q/=qsum
       # Limit to top indices where q > 0 and sum>=0.99? Use topK loops to avoid FFT? For polish, use FFT full maybe.
       g=gradient_weights(p,q)
       g*=n
       g-=g.mean()
       std=g.std()
       if std<1e-12: break
       g=g/std
       for eta in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0]:
           y=np.log(p+1e-300)
           y = y - eta*g
           # sym if p palindromic? If p is symmetric, keep gradient symmetric? g maybe symmetric if p/q symmetric. To enforce pal: y=0.5*(y+y[::-1])
           y-=y.mean(); y=np.clip(y,-8,8)
           cand=softmax(y)
           sc=score_p(cand)
           if sc < best_sc:
               p=cand; best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc; break
       else:
           # random small perturbation
           y=np.log(p+1e-300)+rng.normal(0,0.3)
           y=0.5*(y+y[::-1]); y-=y.mean(); y=clip(-8,8)
           p=softmax(y)
    return best,best_sc
```
Using full gradient via FFT each iter; for n small okay. We can also use topK loops for speed. But full gradient smoother.

Potential issue: `np.log(p+1e-300)` when p min 0 from sym? Use p = np.maximum(p, 1e-15); renormalize before log. Add to mirror.

```
def sample_continuous(q, n):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   # compute z using basis: need cos/sin arrays for n
   z = np.zeros(n)
   for k in 1..K: z += q[2k-2]*np.cos(np.pi*k*x) + q[2k-1]*np.sin(np.pi*k*x)
   p=np.exp(z-n.max); p/=p.sum(); return p
```

       # Sym? optional

```

def random_y_direct(n, rng, mode, scale=1.0):
   if mode % 4 == 0:
      y=rng.normal(0,scale,n)
   elif mode %4 ==1:
      # lowpass Gaussian: generate Fourier random phases
      freq=np.arange(n)
      amp=np.exp(-freq/(n*2.0))*scale
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n)
      y=amp*np.cos(freq*0.1+phase)
   ...
   y-=y.mean(); return clip(y,-6,6)
```

Let's write helper `softmax_y(y)`:
```
def softmax_array(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full_like(y, 1.0/y.size)
    return e/s
```

`normalize_p(p)`:
```
p=np.maximum(np.asarray(p,float),0.0)
s=p.sum()
if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n
return p/s
```

`score_p(p)` uses conv_square and n. For p maybe not sum1; normalize inside? For final candidates, normalize. For optimization, p already normalized. Define:
```
def score_p(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=p.size
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
   p=p/s
   c=conv_square(p)
   return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
```

def lb_value(x):
   n=_LB_N; beta=_LB_BETA
   p=softmax_array(x)
   c=conv_square(p)
   m=np.max(c)
   ex=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
   logsum=np.log(np.sum(ex))
   return float(n*(m + logsum/beta))
```

def cb_values(q, n):
    x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    z = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,_CB_K+1):
        z += q[2*k-2]*np.cos(np.pi*k*x) + q[2*k-1]*np.sin(np.pi*k*x)
    return softmax_array(z)
```

For continuous L-BFGS, `cb_value(q)` uses `_CB_B @ q` precomputed for `_CB_N=4096`. Faster.
```
def cb_value(q):
   z=_CB_B @ q
   p=softmax_array(z)
   c=conv_square(p)
   m=c.max(); beta=_CB_BETA
   ex=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
   return float(_CB_N*(m + np.log(ex.sum())/beta))
def cb_grad(q):
   z=_CB_B @ q
   p=...
   c=...
   ex=...
   q_w=ex/ex.sum()
   gp=gradient_weights(p,q_w)*_CB_N
   a=gp*p
   m=np.sum(a)
   grad = _CB_B.T @ a - np.dot(_CB_B.T @ p, m)
   return grad
```
Note `_CB_B` shape (N,P). `np.dot(_CB_B.T @ p, m)` scalar m times vector. Good.

Mirror polish top-level:
```
def polish_mirror(p, time_limit, rng):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy(); n=p.size; best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   last_improve=t0
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      c=conv_square(p); m=max(c)
      temp=max(m*0.1,1e-12)
      ex=np.exp((c-m)/temp); q=ex/ex.sum()
      gp=gradient_weights(p,q)*n
      gp-=np.mean(gp)
      std=gp.std()
      if std < 1e-14: break
      gp=gp/std
      improved=False
      for eta in [0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]:
          y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
          y=y - eta*gp
          y-=y.mean(); y=np.clip(y,-8,8)
          cand=softmax_array(y)
          sc=score_p(cand)
          if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12):
              p=cand; best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc; improved=True; last_improve=time.monotonic(); break
      if not improved:
          # small random perturb
          y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0,0.25,n)
          y-=y.mean(); y=np.clip(y,-8,8)
          p=softmax_array(y)
          if time.monotonic()-last_improve > min(5.0,time_limit/3):
              # restart from best
              p=best.copy()
              last_improve=time.monotonic()
   return best,best_sc
```
This might be slow for large n if called many; use sparingly. We can skip or use small time.

Direct random y top-level:

def random_y_direct(n, rng, mode, scale):
   if mode ==0: y=rng.normal(0,scale,n)
   elif mode==1:
      # low-frequency via Fourier random
      freq=np.arange(n)
      amp=np.exp(-freq/(n*0.5))*scale
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n)
      y=amp*np.cos(0.7*freq + phase)
   elif mode==2:
      # sampled from continuity-like polynomial random
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      t=rng.normal(0,scale,5)
      y=np.sum(t[j]*(2*x-1)**j for j in range(5))? Can't use generator? Use loop.
   else:
      y=np.log(rng.gamma(1.0+scale, size=n))
   y-=y.mean(); return np.clip(y,-6,6)
```

```
def grid_search_3cos(n):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    c1=np.cos(np.pi*x); c2=np.cos(2*np.pi*x); c3=np.cos(3*np.pi*x)
    best_sc=np.inf; best_y=None
    for a in np.arange(-0.8,0.81,0.1):
      for b in np.arange(-0.6,0.61,0.1):
        for c in np.arange(-0.4,0.41,0.1):
          y=a*c1+b*c2+c*c3
          p=softmax_array(y)
          sc=score_p(p)
          if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_y=y.copy()
    return best_y,best_sc

def downsample_index(arr, m):
   if arr.size == m: return arr.copy()
   if m <=0: return arr.copy()
   if arr.size < m:
       # upsample? not needed
       return np.repeat(arr, max(1,m//arr.size))? maybe fallback
   idx = (np.arange(m) * arr.size // m).astype(int)
   return arr[idx]
```
But downsample by nearest may lose structure. Use block mean if divisible:
```
def downsample_block(arr, m):
   n=arr.size
   if n % m == 0: return arr.reshape(m, n//m).mean(axis=1)
   return downsample_index(arr,m)
```
Use for parent downsample.

Upsample function:
```
def upsample_repeat(p, B):
   if B<=1 or p.size==0: return p
   return np.repeat(p, B) / float(B)
```

```
def random_local_search(p, time_limit, rng):
   p=normalize(p).copy(); best=p; best_sc=score_p(p); base=p; stall=0; sigma=0.15
   while time < limit:
      # whole-array or block
      if rng.random()<0.5:
         cand=base*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
      else:
         width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//8)))
         start=rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width))
         cand=base.copy(); cand[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,width))
      cand=normalize_p(cand)
      sc=score_p(cand)
      if sc<best_sc:
          best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc; base=cand; stall=0
      else:
          stall +=1
          if stall>50:
             base=best.copy(); stall=0
             sigma = min(0.8, sigma*1.2) maybe if sc not too worse? 
   return best,best_sc
```

Let's detail each function.

`normalize_p`:
```
def normalize_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s
```

`softmax_array`:
```
def softmax_array(y):
    y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y=y-np.max(y)
    e=np.exp(y)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(y.shape,1.0/y.size)
    return e/s
```

`conv_square`:
```
def conv_square(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n < 256:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        need=2*n-1
        L=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
        fa=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
        fa[:n]=a
        F=np.fft.rfft(fa)
        c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:need]
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0 and c > -1e-12]? use np.where(np.abs(c)<1e-15,0,c)
    return c
```
Syntax: `c = np.where(np.abs(c) < 1e-15, 0.0, c)`.

`gradient_weights`:
```
def gradient_weights(p,q):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q=np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    q_rev=q[::-1]
    need=3*n-2
    if n < 256:
        conv=np.convolve(p, q_rev)
    else:
        L=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
        P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
        Q=np.fft.rfft(q_rev,L)
        conv=np.fft.irfft(P*Q,L)[:need]
    start=n-1
    stop=2*n-1
    h=conv[start:stop][::-1]
    return 2.0*h
```
For n=1: need=1, L=1, stop=1, start=0, h length1. Good.

`lb_value`:
```
def lb_value(x):
    n=_LB_N
    beta=_LB_BETA
    p=softmax_array(x)
    c=conv_square(p)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    ex=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
    logsum=float(np.log(np.sum(ex)))
    return float(n*(m + logsum/beta))
```
If beta huge, logsum/beta ok. ex sum maybe 0? no.

`lb_grad`:
```
def lb_grad(x):
    n=_LB_N; beta=_LB_BETA
    p=softmax_array(x)
    c=conv_square(p)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    ex=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
    s=ex.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.zeros_like(x)
    q=ex/s
    gp=gradient_weights(p,q)
    gp = gp * float(n)
    a = gp * p
    mfac = float(np.sum(a))
    grad = a - p * mfac
    return grad
```
Note if x length not n, p length x.size; `gp` length n; but assume.

`staged_lbfgs_direct`:
```
def staged_lbfgs_direct(n, y0, time_limit, beta_fractions=(0.25,0.1,0.04,0.015,0.007)):
    global _LB_N
    _LB_N = int(n)
    if time_limit <= 0: ...
    y=np.asarray(y0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()[:n]
    if y.size<n: pad? y0 should correct.
    best_y=y.copy(); best_p=softmax_array(y); best_sc=score_p(best_p)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # maxiter formula
    maxit = max(20, min(150, int(25000/(n+1))))
    bounds=[(-6.0,6.0)]*n
    for frac in beta_fractions:
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
        p=softmax_array(y); c=conv_square(p); m=float(np.max(c)); temp=frac*max(m,1e-15)
        if temp <= 1e-12: break
        global _LB_BETA
        _LB_BETA=1.0/temp
        try:
            res=minimize(lb_value, y, jac=lb_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':10})
        except Exception:
            continue
        if res.x is not None:
            ny=np.nan_to_num(res.x, nan=0.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(ny)):
                ny=np.clip(ny,-6.0,6.0)
                y=ny
                cand=softmax_array(y)
                sc=score_p(cand)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc=sc; best_y=y.copy(); best_p=cand.copy()
    return best_y, best_sc
```
Need `global _LB_BETA` inside; we can assign at top of function `global _LB_N, _LB_BETA`. Do that.

`random_y_direct`:
```
def random_y_direct(n, rng, mode, scale=1.0):
    n=int(n)
    if mode == 0:
        y=rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
    elif mode == 1:
        x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        y=np.zeros(n)
        for k in range(1,6):
            amp=scale*np.exp(-k/3.0)
            y += amp*rng.normal(0.0,1.0)*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    elif mode == 2:
        alpha=0.5+scale
        y=np.log(rng.gamma(alpha, size=n) + 1e-12)
    else:
        x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        y=np.zeros(n)
        for k in range(1,8):
            amp=scale*np.exp(-k/4.0)
            y += amp*rng.normal(0.0,1.0)*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
            y += amp*0.5*rng.normal(0.0,1.0)*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    y-=np.mean(y)
    return np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)
```

`random_local_search`: as above. Use `score_p` exact. Time limit maybe 0. Ensure `n` positive. In block, `width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2, n//8 +1)))`; start `int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))`; if start+width > n adjust. Use `np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,width))`. Normalize.
Stall logic: if no improve for 100, base=best, sigma=min(1.0, sigma*1.25), if sigma>1.0 reset 0.1? We can simple.

`upsample_repeat`: if p sum not 1? Should be. But if p arbitrary, repeating and dividing B keeps sum same. Good.

`downsample_block`: As above.

`cb_value`:
```
def cb_value(q):
    q=np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z=_CB_B @ q
    p=softmax_array(z)
    c=conv_square(p)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    beta=_CB_BETA
    ex=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
    return float(_CB_N*(m + float(np.log(np.sum(ex)))/beta))
```
`cb_grad` similar.

`cb_sample`:
```
def cb_sample(q,n):
    q=np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,_CB_K+1):
        z += q[2*k-2]*np.cos(np.pi*k*x) + q[2*k-1]*np.sin(np.pi*k*x)
    return softmax_array(z)
```
If q shorter? Ensure P length; if not pad. In propose q correct. If n large 4096, loop K=8, fine.

`staged_lbfgs_q`:
```
def staged_lbfgs_q(q0, time_limit, beta_fractions=(0.25,0.1,0.04,0.015,0.007), maxiter=50):
    global _CB_BETA
    P=_CB_B.shape[1]
    q=np.asarray(q0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size<P: q=np.pad(q,(0,P-q.size))
    else: q=q[:P]
    q=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(q,nan=0.0),-5,5)
    best_q=q.copy(); best_sc=1e100
    p=cb_sample(q,_CB_N); best_sc=score_p(p)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    bounds=[(-5.0,5.0)]*P
    maxit=max(10, min(80, int(max(1.0,time_limit)*25.0)))
    for frac in beta_fractions:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       p=cb_sample(q,_CB_N); c=conv_square(p); m=float(np.max(c)); temp=frac*max(m,1e-15)
       if temp <=1e-12: break
       _CB_BETA=1.0/temp
       try: res=minimize(cb_value,q,jac=cb_grad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':10})
       except: continue
       if res.x is not None:
          nq=np.nan_to_num(res.x,nan=0.0)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(nq)):
             nq=np.clip(nq,-5,5)
             q=nq
             p=cb_sample(q,_CB_N); sc=score_p(p)
             if sc<best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_q=q.copy()
    return best_q,best_sc
```
`score_p` uses n=_CB_N? It normalizes p and computes 2n max. Good.

`cb_de_objective`:
```
def cb_de_objective(q):
    q=np.asarray(q,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z=_CBD_B @ q
    p=softmax_array(z)
    c=conv_square(p)
    return float(2.0*_CBD_N*np.max(c))
```

`random_q`:
```
def random_q(rng, scale=1.0):
    P=_CB_B.shape[1]
    decay=np.exp(-np.arange(P, dtype=np.float64)/(1.5*P))
    return rng.normal(0.0, scale*decay, P)
```

`grid_search_3cos`:
```
def grid_search_3cos(n):
    x=(np.arange(int(n))+0.5)/float(n)
    c1=np.cos(np.pi*x); c2=np.cos(2*np.pi*x); c3=np.cos(3*np.pi*x)
    vals_a=np.arange(-0.8,0.81,0.2)
    vals_b=np.arange(-0.6,0.61,0.2)
    vals_c=np.arange(-0.4,0.41,0.2)
    best_sc=np.inf; best_y=None; best_coeffs=(0,0,0)
    for a in vals_a:
      for b in vals_b:
        for c in vals_c:
          y=a*c1+b*c2+c*c3
          p=softmax_array(y)
          sc=score_p(p)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_y=y.copy(); best_coeffs=(float(a),float(b),float(c))
    # if all zero not found? best_y zero
    if best_y is None:
       best_y=np.zeros(n); best_coeffs=(0.0,0.0,0.0); best_sc=score_p(softmax_array(best_y))
    return best_coeffs,best_y,best_sc
```
This may be a bit slow but okay. Values step 0.2. Could include sine in grid? Maybe add a separate grid for a,b,d? But random L-BFGS.

`safe_evaluate`:
```
def safe_evaluate(candidate):
   try:
      vals=[float(x) for x in candidate]
   except Exception:
      return np.inf
   try:
      sc=float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
   except Exception:
      return np.inf
   return sc
```

Let's choose K=8.

Now, let's test gradient formula for continuous with `_CB_B` basis missing constant. If q all zero, p uniform. Gradient from `cb_grad`: uniform p, qweights triangular, gp = n*gradient_weights. `a=gp*p`; grad = B.T a - B.T p * sum(a). It will be zero if uniform? Let's see if p uniform and qweights symmetric? gp maybe? Not necessarily due triangular qweights; uniform f might be critical for **full max surrogate**? It might not be zero because central coefficients dominated; gradient should push p. Good.

Now, direct L-BFGS from continuous sample: The p is smooth, but softmax y range may be ~[-3,3]. Bounds [-6,6]. Good.

One potential mathematical issue: The objective function internal score uses `conv_square(p)` = linear convolution p*p. For p from continuous sample with midpoint, c_k corresponds to sums of indices; for k near n/2 etc. Good.

Let's think of finite-n edge c0 and c_{2n-2}. If continuous f has high endpoint values, p_0 and p_{n-1} maybe high, causing edge coefficients p0^2 and p_{n-1}^2. If f endpoint high ~O(1), c0~1/n^2, negligible vs 1/n. If f has mass concentrated at endpoint, not continuous. So okay.

    p = normalize_p(p).copy()
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy()
    best_sc=score_p(p)
    base=p.copy()
    sigma=0.1
    stall=0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        cand=base.copy()
        if n > 0:
           if rng.random() < 0.6:
              cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
           else:
              width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//10)))
              if width > n: width=n
              start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))

        cand=normalize_p(cand)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2 * n * max_k conv(a, a)[k] / (sum(a)**2).

The implementation uses several complementary ideas:
  * continuous density optimization in a centered Fourier basis,
  * multi-start annealed L-BFGS-B optimization on the discrete simplex,
  * a coarse trigonometric parametrization and small direct searches,
  * final multiplicative local search / candidate polishing.

The exact evaluator is only used for final candidate checks; internal
objective computation is equivalent but vectorized with FFT.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_p(p):
    """Normalize to a non-negative sequence of sum 1 (or uniform fallback)."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def softmax_array(y):
    """Numerically stable softmax, returns sum 1."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(y.shape, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def conv_square(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        need = 2 * n - 1
        L = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
        fa = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        fa[:n] = a
        F = np.fft.rfft(fa)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.abs(c) < 1e-15, 0.0, c)
    return c


def score_p(p):
    """Internal score: 2*n*max(conv(p,p)) for normalized p."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_square(p)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def gradient_weights(p, q):
    """
    For weights q_k, compute
        2 * sum_k q_k * d(conv(p,p)_k)/d p_i
    = 2 * sum_k q_k p_{k-i} over valid i.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    q_rev = q[::-1]
    need = 3 * n - 2
    if n < 256:
        conv = np.convolve(p, q_rev)
    else:
        L = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        Q = np.fft.rfft(q_rev, L)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(P * Q, L)[:need]
    start = n - 1
    stop = 2 * n - 1
    h = conv[start:stop][::-1]
    return 2.0 * h


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Direct discrete L-BFGS-B optimization in softmax coordinates.
# Globals are used because scipy.optimize.minimize requires top-level functions.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

_LB_N = 1
_LB_BETA = 1.0


def lb_value(x):
    n = _LB_N
    beta = _LB_BETA
    p = softmax_array(x)
    c = conv_square(p)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    ex = np.exp(beta * (c - m))
    logsum = float(np.log(np.sum(ex)))
    return float(n * (m + logsum / beta))


def lb_grad(x):
    n = _LB_N
    beta = _LB_BETA
    p = softmax_array(x)
    c = conv_square(p)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    ex = np.exp(beta * (c - m))
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    q = ex / s
    gp = gradient_weights(p, q)
    gp = gp * float(n)
    a = gp * p
    fac = float(np.sum(a))
    return a - p * fac


def staged_lbfgs_direct(n, y0, time_limit,
                        beta_fractions=(0.25, 0.10, 0.04, 0.015, 0.007)):
    """Annealed L-BFGS-B on y=log p for a fixed length n."""
    global _LB_N, _LB_BETA
    n = int(n)
    _LB_N = n
    y = np.asarray(y0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size < n:
        y = np.pad(y, (0, n - y.size), mode="edge")
    y = y[:n]
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)
    y -= float(np.mean(y))

    best_y = y.copy()
    best_p = softmax_array(y)
    best_sc = score_p(best_p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    maxit = max(20, min(150, int(25000 / (n + 1))))
    bounds = [(-6.0, 6.0)] * n
    opts = {"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-10, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 10}

    for frac in beta_fractions:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        p = softmax_array(y)
        c = conv_square(p)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        temp = frac * max(m, 1e-15)
        if temp <= 1e-12:
            break
        _LB_BETA = 1.0 / temp
        try:
            res = minimize(lb_value, y, jac=lb_grad,
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if res.x is not None:
            ny = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64),
                               nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(ny)):
                ny = np.clip(ny, -6.0, 6.0)
                ny -= float(np.mean(ny))
                y = ny
                p = softmax_array(y)
                sc = score_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_y = y.copy()
                    best_p = p.copy()
    return best_y, best_sc


def random_y_direct(n, rng, mode, scale=1.0):
    """Random smooth / rough initial log-weights."""
    n = int(n)
    if mode == 0:
        y = rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
    elif mode == 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 6):
            amp = scale * np.exp(-k / 3.0)
            y += (amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) *
                  np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
    elif mode == 2:
        alpha = 0.5 + max(0.1, float(scale))
        y = np.log(rng.gamma(alpha, size=n) + 1e-12)
    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 9):
            amp = scale * np.exp(-k / 4.0)
            y += (amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) *
                  np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
            y += (0.5 * amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) *
                  np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    return np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)


def random_local_search(p, time_limit, rng):
    """Multiplicative random local search in the simplex."""
    p = normalize_p(p).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_p(p)
    base = p.copy()
    sigma = 0.10
    stall = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        cand = base.copy()
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.55:
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
        else:
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 200), max(2, n // 8 + 1)))
            if width > n:
                width = max(1, n)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, stop - start))
        cand = normalize_p(cand)
        sc = score_p(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_p = cand.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            base = cand.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 80:
                base = best_p.copy()
                sigma = min(0.9, sigma * 1.25)
            elif stall == 180:
                base = best_p.copy()
                sigma = 0.10
            elif stall == 320:
                base = best_p.copy()
                sigma = 0.04
    return best_p, best_sc


def upsample_repeat(p, B):
    """Repeat each coefficient B times and renormalize."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    B = int(B)
    if B <= 1:
        return p
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    return np.repeat(p, B) / float(B)


def downsample_block(arr, m):
    """Block average if divisible, otherwise nearest-index downsampling."""
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(m)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or m <= 0:
        return arr.copy()
    if m >= n:
        return arr.copy()
    if n % m == 0:
        return arr.reshape(m, n // m).mean(axis=1)
    idx = (np.arange(m) * n // m).astype(int)
    return arr[idx]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Continuous density basis:
#   log f(x) ~ sum_j q_j phi_j(x), phi_j centered Fourier terms.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

_CB_K = 8
_CB_N = 2048
_CB_BETA = 1.0

_CB_X = (np.arange(_CB_N) + 0.5) / float(_CB_N)
_CB_parts = []
for _k in range(1, _CB_K + 1):
    _CB_parts.append(np.cos(np.pi * _k * _CB_X))
    _CB_parts.append(np.sin(np.pi * _k * _CB_X))
_CB_B = np.column_stack(_CB_parts)
_CB_B -= _CB_B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)


_CBD_N = 1024
_CBD_X = (np.arange(_CBD_N) + 0.5) / float(_CBD_N)
_CBD_parts = []
for _k in range(1, _CB_K + 1):
    _CBD_parts.append(np.cos(np.pi * _k * _CBD_X))
    _CBD_parts.append(np.sin(np.pi * _k * _CBD_X))
_CBD_B = np.column_stack(_CBD_parts)
_CBD_B -= _CBD_B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)


def cb_sample(q, n):
    """Sample the continuous density at n points."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    P = _CB_B.shape[1]
    if q.size < P:
        q = np.pad(q, (0, P - q.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        q = q[:P]
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, _CB_K + 1):
        z += q[2 * k - 2] * np.cos(np.pi * k * x)
        z += q[2 * k - 1] * np.sin(np.pi * k * x)
    return softmax_array(z)


def cb_value(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = _CB_B @ q
    p = softmax_array(z)
    c = conv_square(p)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    beta = _CB_BETA
    ex = np.exp(beta * (c - m))
    logsum = float(np.log(np.sum(ex)))
    return float(_CB_N * (m + logsum / beta))


def cb_grad(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    P = _CB_B.shape[1]
    if q.size < P:
        q = np.pad(q, (0, P - q.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        q = q[:P]
    z = _CB_B @ q
    p = softmax_array(z)
    c = conv_square(p)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    beta = _CB_BETA
    ex = np.exp(beta * (c - m))
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
    qw = ex / s
    gp = gradient_weights(p, qw)
    gp = gp * float(_CB_N)
    a = gp * p
    fac = float(np.sum(a))
    return _CB_B.T @ a - (_CB_B.T @ p) * fac


def staged_lbfgs_q(q0, time_limit,
                   beta_fractions=(0.25, 0.10, 0.04, 0.015, 0.007)):
    """Annealed L-BFGS-B in continuous basis coordinates."""
    global _CB_BETA
    P = _CB_B.shape[1]
    q = np.asarray(q0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size < P:
        q = np.pad(q, (0, P - q.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        q = q[:P]
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, -5.0, 5.0)

    best_q = q.copy()
    best_p = cb_sample(q, _CB_N)
    best_sc = score_p(best_p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    maxit = max(15, min(80, int(max(1.0, time_limit) * 25.0)))
    bounds = [(-5.0, 5.0)] * P
    opts = {"maxiter": int(maxit), "ftol": 1e-10, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 10}

    for frac in beta_fractions:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        p = cb_sample(q, _CB_N)
        c = conv_square(p)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        temp = frac * max(m, 1e-15)
        if temp <= 1e-12:
            break
        _CB_BETA = 1.0 / temp
        try:
            res = minimize(cb_value, q, jac=cb_grad,
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if res.x is not None:
            nq = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64),
                               nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(nq)):
                nq = np.clip(nq, -5.0, 5.0)
                q = nq
                p = cb_sample(q, _CB_N)
                sc = score_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_q = q.copy()
                    best_p = p.copy()
    return best_q, best_sc, best_p


def cb_de_objective(q):
    """Exact score on a smaller grid, used for global DE search."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = _CBD_B @ q
    p = softmax_array(z)
    c = conv_square(p)
    return float(2.0 * _CBD_N * float(np.max(c)))


def random_q(rng, scale=1.0):
    """Random Fourier parameters with slight low-frequency bias."""
    P = _CB_B.shape[1]
    decay = np.exp(-np.arange(P, dtype=np.float64) / max(1.0, 1.5 * P))
    return rng.normal(0.0, max(0.0, float(scale)) * decay, P)


def grid_search_3cos(n):
    """
    Coarse search over y = a cos(pi x) + b cos(2 pi x) + c cos(3 pi x).
    Produces a reliable starting point.
    """
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    c1 = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    c2 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    c3 = np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)

    vals_a = np.arange(-0.8, 0.81, 0.2)
    vals_b = np.arange(-0.6, 0.61, 0.2)
    vals_c = np.arange(-0.4, 0.41, 0.2)

    best_sc = float("inf")
    best_y = None
    best_coeffs = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    for a in vals_a:
        for b in vals_b:
            for cc in vals_c:
                y = a * c1 + b * c2 + cc * c3
                y -= float(np.mean(y))
                p = softmax_array(y)
                sc = score_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_y = y.copy()
                    best_coeffs = (float(a), float(b), float(cc))

    if best_y is None:
        best_y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = score_p(softmax_array(best_y))
    return best_coeffs, best_y, best_sc


def safe_evaluate(candidate):
    """Call the provided exact evaluator safely."""
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in candidate]
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    return sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Main function invoked by the judge.
    Returns a list of non-negative floats with the best discovered score.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_p = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    good_q = []
    coeff_list = []
    priority_inits = []
    extra_inits = []

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Use the provided starting construction if useful.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(parent.sum()) > 0.0:
            ppar = normalize_p(parent)
            sc = score_p(ppar)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_p = ppar.copy()

            for m in [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]:
                if parent.size >= m:
                    pd = downsample_block(parent, m)
                    pd = normalize_p(pd)
                    sc = score_p(pd)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_p = pd.copy()
                    if pd.size >= 64:
                        y = np.log(np.maximum(pd, 1e-300))
                        y -= float(np.mean(y))
                        priority_inits.append((m, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_sc = score_p(best_p)

    print(f"[init] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f} len={best_p.size}", flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Coarse trigonometric grid: cheap and often gives a good basin.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0:
        coeffs, yg, scg = grid_search_3cos(1024)
        if coeffs is not None and np.isfinite(scg):
            coeff_list.append(coeffs)
            p = softmax_array(yg)
            sc = score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
            y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            y -= float(np.mean(y))
            priority_inits.append((1024, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))

            for m in [2048, 4096]:
                x = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
                y = (coeffs[0] * np.cos(np.pi * x) +
                     coeffs[1] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) +
                     coeffs[2] * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x))
                y -= float(np.mean(y))
                y = np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)
                priority_inits.append((m, y))
                p = softmax_array(y)
                sc = score_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()
        print(f"[grid] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Continuous Fourier basis phase.
    # Leaves time for direct discrete refinement.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    cont_deadline = deadline - (300.0 if budget > 1200.0 else 100.0)
    if cont_deadline > t0 + 60.0:
        P = _CB_B.shape[1]
        q0 = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
        if coeff_list:
            cc = coeff_list[0]
            q0[0] = cc[0]
            if P > 2:
                q0[2] = cc[1]
            if P > 4:
                q0[4] = cc[2]
            q0 = np.clip(q0, -5.0, 5.0)

        qb, sq, pb = staged_lbfgs_q(q0, 18.0)
        if qb is not None:
            p4 = cb_sample(qb, 4096)
            sc4 = score_p(p4)
            if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc4
                best_p = p4.copy()
            if np.isfinite(sq):
                good_q.append(qb)
                y = np.log(np.maximum(p4, 1e-300))
                y -= float(np.mean(y))
                priority_inits.append((4096, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))

        # Global DE on a smaller grid for the Fourier parameters.
        if time.monotonic() < cont_deadline - 60.0:
            try:
                bounds_de = [(-5.0, 5.0)] * P
                de_res = differential_evolution(
                    cb_de_objective,
                    bounds_de,
                    maxiter=70,
                    popsize=15,
                    tol=1e-3,
                    xtol=1e-3,
                    seed=int(seed),
                    init="sobol",
                    polish=False,
                )
                if de_res.x is not None:
                    qde = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(de_res.x, dtype=np.float64),
                                        nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                    qde = np.clip(qde, -5.0, 5.0)
                    qde, sde, pde_small = staged_lbfgs_q(qde, 12.0)
                    pde = cb_sample(qde, 4096)
                    scde = score_p(pde)
                    if np.isfinite(scde) and scde < best_sc:
                        best_sc = scde
                        best_p = pde.copy()
                    if np.isfinite(sde):
                        good_q.append(qde)
                        y = np.log(np.maximum(pde, 1e-300))
                        y -= float(np.mean(y))
                        priority_inits.append((4096, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Random restarts in Fourier basis.
        scales = [0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4, 0.2, 0.8, 1.4, 0.5, 2.0]
        r = 0
        while time.monotonic() < cont_deadline:
            r += 1
            if good_q and r % 4 == 0:
                base_q = good_q[int(rng.integers(0, len(good_q)))]
                q0 = base_q + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, P)
                q0 = np.clip(q0, -5.0, 5.0)
            else:
                q0 = random_q(rng, scales[(r - 1) % len(scales)])

            maxt = 2.2 if r < 25 else 1.6
            remaining = cont_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if maxt > remaining:
                maxt = max(0.0, remaining)
            if maxt <= 0.2:
                break

            try:
                qb, sq, pb = staged_lbfgs_q(q0, maxt)
            except Exception:
                continue

            if qb is not None:
                p4 = cb_sample(qb, 4096)
                sc4 = score_p(p4)
                if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc4
                    best_p = p4.copy()
                    print(f"[cont r={r}] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f} len=4096", flush=True)

                if np.isfinite(sq) and sq < best_sc + 0.12:
                    good_q.append(qb)
                    if len(good_q) > 14:
                        good_q = good_q[:14]

                if bad := (np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc + 0.05):
                    y = np.log(np.maximum(p4, 1e-300))
                    y -= float(np.mean(y))
                    priority_inits.append((4096, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))

    print(f"[cont] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f} len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build direct discrete initializations from the best continuous objects.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    for q in good_q[:10]:
        if q is None:
            continue
        for m in [1024, 2048, 4096]:
            try:
                p = cb_sample(q, m)
                y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                y -= float(np.mean(y))
                priority_inits.append((m, np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # A few direct random smooth starts as a safety net.
    for m in [256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]:
        for mode in range(3):
            try:
                y = random_y_direct(m, rng, mode, scale=1.1)
                extra_inits.append((m, y))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Direct discrete L-BFGS-B refinement.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    direct_deadline = deadline - 180.0
    direct_items = priority_inits + extra_inits
    seen_len_mode = set()

    for m, y in direct_items:
        if time.monotonic() > direct_deadline:
            break
        if m is None or y is None:
            continue
        m = int(m)
        if m < 64 or m > 4096:
            continue
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if y.size != m:
            if y.size < m:
                y = np.pad(y, (0, m - y.size), mode="edge")
            else:
                y = y[:m]
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        y = np.clip(y, -6.0, 6.0)
        y -= float(np.mean(y))

        # Avoid too many duplicate long tasks for the same length.
        key = m
        cnt = sum(1 for mm, _ in direct_items if mm == m)
        if key in seen_len_mode and cnt > 18:
            continue
        seen_len_mode.add(key)

        if m <= 256:
            tl = 3.0
        elif m <= 512:
            tl = 4.0
        elif m <= 1024:
            tl = 6.0
        elif m <= 2048:
            tl = 8.0
        else:
            tl = 10.0

        remaining = direct_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if tl > remaining:
            tl = max(0.0, remaining)
        if tl <= 0.5:
            break

        try:
            yb, sb = staged_lbfgs_direct(m, y, tl)
        except Exception:
            continue

        if yb is not None:
            p = softmax_array(yb)
            sc = score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                print(f"[direct n={m}] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    print(f"[direct] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f} len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final multiplicative local search on the best discrete sequence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    local_deadline = deadline - 25.0
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
        tl = min(300.0, max(5.0, local_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        try:
            ploc, scloc = random_local_search(best_p, tl, rng)
            if ploc is not None and np.isfinite(scloc) and scloc < best_sc:
                best_sc = scloc
                best_p = ploc.copy()
                print(f"[local] best internal score = {best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final candidate list and exact evaluation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(p):
        if p is None:
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            return
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        p = normalize_p(p)
        if float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
            candidates.append(p)

    add_candidate(best_p)
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size > 0:
        add_candidate(upsample_repeat(best_p, 2))
        if best_p.size * 4 <= 8192:
            add_candidate(upsample_repeat(best_p, 4))
        # symmetrized variant, sometimes finite-n helpful
        add_candidate(0.5 * (best_p + best_p[::-1]))

    # Continuous Fourier best objects.
    for q in good_q[:4]:
        try:
            add_candidate(cb_sample(q, 4096))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Grid coefficient samples.
    for coeffs in coeff_list[:3]:
        for m in [2048, 4096]:
            try:
                x = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
                y = (coeffs[0] * np.cos(np.pi * x) +
                     coeffs[1] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) +
                     coeffs[2] * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x))
                add_candidate(softmax_array(y))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Parent, if any, as a fallback-ish candidate.
    try:
        add_candidate(parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Guaranteed valid fallback.
    add_candidate(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)

    final_p = None
    final_sc = float("inf")

    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            sc = safe_evaluate(cand)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_sc:
            final_sc = sc
            final_p = cand

    if final_p is None:
        final_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        try:
            final_sc = safe_evaluate(final_p)
        except Exception:
            final_sc = float("inf")

    print(f"[final] exact score = {final_sc:.10f} len={final_p.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
